GARGAD'I....banyarda wani ko wata ba yajuyamin novel takowace siga"idan kunne yaji jiki....

GODIYA... alhamdullh ala kully halin tsira da amincin Allah su tabbata ga shugabanmu annabi Muhammad(S A W) yah Allah yadda nafara wannan book lfy Allah kabani lfy da rai nagamashi lfy.

*BISMILLAHIR RAHAMANIR RAHIM*

 Dedicata to all members of my grps🥰💞💔 inayinku guys💃


🅿�? 1&2

Kano state G R A
........k'was k'was take takowa acikin parlourn" tafito cikin shirinta na d'aukar hankali"tana zuba wani fitinan nan k'amshi.sanye take da riga da siket na kanti"tayafa gyale da zamu iya kira dashi gara babu"siket d'in yakamata sosai ya fito mata da sharf.yyinda rigar me gajeran hannu ce"dg gaban rigar ta bud'e"har ana hango shatin nashanunta dasuka cika rigar...tayi parking bak'in gashinta"da wanda baisaniba zaiyi zaton attached ne..fuskarta tayi light make up bamai hayaniya ba...SALMA kenan da akema lak'abi da ( baby)"yarinya me tashe da gayu"tana tak'ama da kyawunta da iliminta.kallo d'aya zaka mata kasan ta amsa sunanta *classic lady*
Tana rik'e da hand bag nata da key d'in motarta....ummi dake zaune kan kujera ta dubeta tana girgiza kanta"wannan shigar da y'ar tata keyi yana k'ona ranta"fuska a'daure tadubeta tace".wato anan ma d'in irin wannan shigar zaki dingayi ko?bazaki yiwa kanki fad'a ba dan Allah baby?yanzunma Ina zakije da wannan shigar?tana turo baki tace".amma ummi ni miye aibun shigata dan Allah?harara ummi tamata had'e da cewa bansaniba! marar kunyar banza"kina zubar mana da mutunci agari"kinsanfa a *katsina*
haka kike wannan shigar banzar ko?shine anan ma dg dawowarmu shekaran jiya zaki koma da halinki ko?haba ummi"zamaninku daban namu daban"kinsan banwani san garin nan ba"zanje gidansu raudah!ta nunamun gari ne...dan zakije shine zakiyi irin wannan shigar banzar?"to wlh indai kinason fita kije kisaka hijab koki canza kaya"idan ba hakaba baki zuwa ko Ina...

shagwab'e fuska tayi had'e da cewa"nifa ummi wlh ana takurani agidan nan" kinsan ni nafison k'ananun Kaya...

      gidanku nace marar kunyar banza"sokike alh yadawo yaganki kinfita ahaka yayita fad'a ko?

baby batace komai ba ta juya zuwa upstairs"

     babu jumawa tadawo sanye da hijab har k'asa"ummi na murmushi tace".yauwa autana kokefa"amma irin wannan shigar da kikeyi beda amfani saikiga wasu namiki wani ZARGI daban...

Baby batace komai ba ta fice dg parlourn"

    tana fitowa ta wuce parking space ta d'auki motarta"saidai tana shiga motar taja tsaki had'e da cire hijab d'in ta yafa mayafinta..

Ahankali take driving nata cikin nutsuwa da aji"ta nufi anguwar hotoro"gidan yayan raudah k'awarta.. sbd ita anguwar tafi sani acikin garin kano...

   *wacece baby*?

Alh buhari kurfi!shine mahaifin baby"asalinsa d'an k'auyan kurfine dake jihar KT"danginsa na can shikuma a KT suke da zama da matarsa hjy maryam"amma anmishi transfer agun aikinsa na kwangilar gine gine..yadawo kano da zama shida iyalansa.yanada yara 4" yaya bilal,yaya Ahmed sai salma(baby)wacce taci sunan mahaifiyar alh buhari...sai k'aninta usman"
baby yariny ce y'ar kimanin 24 yrs"ta kammala karatunta a b'angaren pharmacy"bata fara aikiba sai monday mai zuwa"sbd aminin abbanta 
*alh nuhu*
yasamar mata aiki awata private hospital anan garin kano"baby yarinya ce  maison gayu da wayewa"hakan yasa ita aganinta duk shigar dazatayi bata ganin aibun shigar..tanada son saka kaya k'anana..bakuma wai dan batada ilimi ba"ah ah tayi saukar alk'ur'ani mai girma"alokacin dasuke KT da yawa maza namata maganar banza sbd shigar datakeyi"amma bata kulasu,sbd tatsani namiji d'an iska"kusan duk yawanci abinda ke kawo samarin nata kenan...Amma ita takasa gane shigar banzar datakeyi kekawo mata hakan"shiyasa har yanzun takasa saurayi tsayayye..

Baby Fara ce !amma bacanba"tanada k'ira me d'aukar hankali"yarinyace ita dabata d'aukar raini"tanada hak'uri..amma idan aka tab'ota"wayyo Allah..

Abba da Yaya Bilal Babu irin duka da zagin dabasuyi mata ba.sbd tadena amma bata fasawa"idanma ance tasaka hijab data fita zata cire idan tadawo tasaka ta shigo gida.
Alh buhari bayada aminin daya wuce alh nuhu tun k'uruciya"kuma agari guda kurfi suke"bayan sun gama karatu kowane yazauna agarin da aka turasa aiki yana aiki"matayansu sunsan juna" saidai yaranne nasu basu san junaba.sbd ba gari d'aya ba!kuma anajima wa ba'a yi wata hidimaba..sai gashi Allah yasa anyowa alh buhari transfer zuwa garin kano.hakan yayiwa alh nuhu dad'i sosai...

Raudah k'awar baby ce tun a KT"kasancewar sunyi secondary skul atare"raudah na anan kano gidan yayanta zaune yana rik'onta.tana kuma karatunta"shine baby tasanar mata sundawo anan da zama"takumazo taga gidan sunje da baby taga gidansu"to kuma yanzun shine sukayi awaya taje tad'auketa su fita shak'atawa taga Yaya garin yake.....

Cigaban labarin

Cikin nutsuwa take tafiya tasaki kid'a na Musik"

Tana k'ok'ari shiga corner d'in da zata sadata da layin..

Wata had'add'iyar mota ta banko da gudu... duk yadda taso ta kaucewa motar kasawa tayi"

 saida motar tadaki tata motar"tana salati ta tsaida motar...jikake kasssssss!k'arar glass d'in fitilar gaban motar ya tsage...wasu mutum  3 duk suka tsaya abakin hanyar suna kallonsu.

bayan tasamu ta tsaida motar"tajira taga me motar yajanye tasamu ta wuce"

Amma shiru bbu alamar hakan"

sannan motar me tintak ce"baya ganin naciki saidai naciki yaga na waje...

Motar daba'aganin na ciki na duba"wasu matasan mazajene su2 aciki...wanda ke zaune driver sit nakai dubana agaresa....Masha Allah!irinsu ake kira one in million...loll.. had'add'an guy ne"daya amsa sunansa namiji"kallo d'aya zaka masa kaga tsantsar aji"wayewa dakuma hutu had'e da ilimin zamani atare dashi... fararen idanuwansa yaware yana kallon motar baby"
Cikin tsananin fusata da fushi DR HAKEEM yakalli abokinsa dake zaune kusa dashi"cikin dakakkiyar muryarsabme cike da haiba yace".mus'ab me hakan ke nufi?"tana jira ne saina janye ta wuce kokuwa ita bazata janye mu wuceba??"
Mus'ab yace".nikuwa big man taya zan iya ganewa?

Kafin Dr Hakeem yyi mgn baby ta fito dg cikin motar ta"cikin tsananin b'acin rai.

Aganinta kosuwaye sungama raina Mata hankali"subige Mata mota,suyi Mata b'anna"amma bazasu bata hanya ta wuceba kosu b'ata hak'uri....

Sosai mus'ab ke kallonta harta iso adede b'angaren da Dr Hakeem ke zaune ta fara musu knocking da k'arfi"tana mgn kamar haka...

Kokai wane wawane  ya Isa kajanye k'ajagar motarka kabawa mutane hanya su wuce"kamun b'anna kafi k'arfin kabani hak'uri....kafin ta rufe baki anturo k'ofar motar da k'arfi...✍️

🅿�? 3&4

""santala santalan k'afafunsa dake sanye da takalmi sawu ciki yafara zurowa waje.yana binta da wani kallo na k'yama ya Ida fitowa"tana tsaye gefe fuskarta bbu alamar annuri acikinta"K``yafad'a cikin raini da wulak'anci"nafi k'arfin natsaya dake Ina mgn amatsayinki na k'aruwa!wacce ke tallar jikinta.look at you! yafad'a yana nunata da yatsa"kin Isa ki kirani da wawa"bacin kina k'aramar y'ar bariki....cikin tsananin b'acin rai da haushin kalaman sa"baby ta matso tace".kaine dai d'an bariki baniba"kuma sai Allah yasakamun akan kirana karuwa dakayi wlh....haba big man!ba girmanka da mutuncinka bane" kana fad'i in fad'a da mace...kuduba yadda har jama'a sun fara taruwa ana kallonku"cewar mus'ab yana shiga atsakkiyar su"mittssw!! baby tadoka tsaki"mus'ab yace".pls& pls kuyi hakuri kun sami mis   under standing ne shiyasa...malam gafara kabani waje nazubar mata da hak'oranta"wawiya k'azama marar kunyar k'arya"inhar ka cika d'an halak ba shegeba kazubar mun da hak'oran mana..baby tafad'a tana mishi wanin mugun kallo"ture mus'ab Dr Hakeem yakeyi"amma mus'ab yarik'e sa sosai" sbd yasan halin mutumin nashi"da bak'ar zuciya uwa kuturu."wlh duk inda nakoma had'uwa dake kiyi ta kanki"cewar Dr Hakeem yana huci"mus'ab nabinta da wani kallo yace".pls hjy you can go!harara ta watsawa mus'ab taja tsaki had'e da cewa" akoma islamiyya dan Allah! k'aton gardi dakai katsaya kana sa'insa da mace...wani kukan kura Dr Hakeem yayi, yafisge hannuwansa dg na mus'ab"yadira gaban baby dake k'ok'arin bud'e mota ta shiga....

Tana juyowa ya d'auketa da wani lafiyayyen mari"

Tasaki k'ara tana dafe kuncinta"hakan yyi daidai da k'arasowar mus'ab yarikesa da wani mutum"

Allah ya'isa banyafe makaba"sai Allah yabimun hakk'ina"wawa wanda baisan darajan d'an Adam ba"

    Tana fad'in hakan ta bud'e motarta ta shiga"taja baya da gudu tazo ta daki motarsa"atake itama glass d'in fitilar motar sukayi tsaga...

Maimakon ta wuce inda tayi niyar zuwa.kawai saita canza akalar motar zuwa gida.tafasa zuwa hotoron"

Dr Hakeem kuwa da k'yar mus'ab yasakashi amota.

    Yakuma sha alwashin bazata zagesa taci bulus ba"ko ita y'ar waye"inhar yakoma had'uwa da ita saita raina kanta"

Yanayin da mus'ab yaganshi aciki yasaka ya karb'i driving d'in"

Muje ka mayar dani gida kawai"Dr Hakeem yafad'a idanuwansa sunyi jajir..kafasa zuwa asibitin?

   Eh nafasa"

shiru kawai mus'ab yyi yanufi anguwar y'an kaba...

babu me mgn har suka iso gidansu Dr Hakeem"zuciyarsa na k'una had'e da suya"abinda yafaru kawai ne aransa yakasa yamanta"jiyake inama ace yasan k'aruwar nan(k'alu bale gareku mata masu shigar banza da fitar da suran jikinsu.gashi dai baby an lik'a mata sunan karuwa ) parking d'in da mus'ab yayi yasakashi dawowa tunaninsa".

Atare suka fito"mus'ab yace".kayi hakuri big man akan abinda yafaru kaida yarinyar nan"nina wuce saimun had'u.bejira cewarsaba ya nufi bakin get yafice.

Dr Hakeem ya wuce k'ofar dazata sadaka da shiga interest d'in shiga gidan..

Can cikin mak'oshi yayi sallamar"
  
    Ammi dake zaune kan kujera tana lazimin marece ta amsa tana binsa da kallo"atake tagano ransa ab'ace yake.saidai tayi zaton zuhura ce sanadin b'acin ransa"(matarsa da basufi 2 yrs da aureba)

Ammi sannu da gida!yauwa ya aikin? alhmdllh yafad'a atakaice yana zama kan kujera"yace"Ina nafeesa?suna islamiyya sunkusa tasowa ai yanzun"meye ammi kika dafa?"

Tuwon semonvita miyar kubewa busassa.😋

Tashi tsaye yyi ya wuce dining area d'in"ajiyar zuciya tasaki tana tunani aranta gsky bazata kashe rayuwar d'anta ba.ace anmishi aure sbd ya huta yasami kulawa" amma bbu ita"kullum anan yakeyin breakfast yyi lunch"dinner kawai yace".zuhura ke girkamasa"kuma ammi nazaton k'arya yakeyi"itafa inbacin da yanace da zuhura yakeso wlh dabazai auretaba.gaba d'aya yarinyar itadai bata mataba"sannan gashi harsun yi 2 yrs amma bbu haihuwa....bara alh yadawo sunyi mgn gsky anemi mafita....sallamar nafeesa ta katse mata tunani"amsawa tayi tana cewa kuntaso?"eh ammi "Yaya sannu da hutawa! tafad'a tana kallon sashen dayake zaune..da hannu yamata alamar tazo...bbu musu ta nufi gunsa"itama kallo d'aya tamasa tagano akwai wani abu dake damunsa"zama tayi gefen sa"Yayana Ina wuni?lfy qlau !jiya da yau bangankaba  sai yanzun"cikin gundura da surutunt  a yace".idan kinso ganina kije gidana ko office d'ina mana"zaro ido tayi had'e da cewa Allah yatsareni da zuwa gidanka.... wannan anty zuhuran da bbu ruwanta da mutane bbu sakin fuska"mutum yaje amishi banza...katseta yyi da cewa"kinga kaina naciwo kije kikamun ruwa saiki koma parlour..bata wani ji haushi ba, sbd tasaba da halinsa"anama ganin yafi sakewa da ita akan kowa"kuma ayanzun ta lura ransa ab'ace yake...

    bayan ta kamishi ruwan tabar gurin"shikuwa saida aka fara kiran sallar magrib yafita dg gidan...

*Wanene Dr Hakeem*?

Kamar yadda kukaji tarihin alh buhari kurfi"shima haka alh nuhu kurfi..banbancinsu su dama agarin Kano suke dazama"alh nuhu da matarsa hjy ruk'ayya(ammi)sunada y'ay'a 5"Abdul Hakeem shine babba"sai Abdallah sannan Fatima da anmata aure da yaranta 2"sai nafeesa da Faisal..

suna zaune cikin had'inkai dason zumunci"

ABDUL HAKEEM NUHU matashin saurayine d'an kimanin shekara 36 yrs"kamilin namijine shi"Mai izza da miskilanci, had'e da aji"Dr Hakeem dogone wankan tarwad'a'.shiba siriri ba bakuma me jikiba"yanada k'irar ingarman namiji"bayada yawan mgn"kuma baya d'aukar raini da wulak'anci..yanada kwarjini da Kamala"kana ganinsa kasan ya amsa sunansa namiji..shine irin namijin da kowace mace ke mafarkin samu.akwaishi da shan k'amshi"fuskarnan ko kullum a had'e tamkar bai tab'a sanin an halicci fara'a ba.kallo d'aya zaka masa kasan hankalinsa a kwance take"hakanan ko Kai d'an gidan uban waye in har kashiga harkarsa to bazai k'yaleba .fitinanne ne nak'in k'arawa..babban likitan matane me farin jini"abokansa da y'an matan Kano namasa lak'ani da BIG MAN...ya kammala karatunsa na likita"a Oxford London yak'arasa karatunsa na medicine,sannan yak'aro da residency yazama had'add'an constltant a fannin Mata .wato (Gyanaecologist)bayan yadawo gida Nigeria"ko Ina nemansa akeyi yayi aiki..kasancewar takardunsa sunyi kyau"governor yabashi umarnin yayi aiki awata general hospital hospital dake agarin Kano sbd taimakon al'umma.sannan mahaifinsa yagina mishi tashi asibitin sa"dandanan yazuba k'wararrun ma'aikata..dawowarsa  da shekara 2 da Fara aiki ya auri zuhura..wacce itace da kanta ta cusa kanta agunsa.yana disgata damata wulak'ancin har Allah yasa tasamu wani lak'ani agun boka tashafa aidonta dg nan yafara k'ok'arin amincewa da soyayyar ta.gaba d'aya iyayensa bawanison aurensa da zuhura sukeba.sbd bata bashi wata kulawa"gefe guda kuma Dr Hakeem yasan aminin mahaifinsa alh buhari"sau d'aya yatab'a zuwa KT yagaidosa"kusan 10 yrs kenan alokacin kuma baby tana bourding skul..Yaya Bilal kawai yasani ayaran gidan...

Zuhura aminu mai k'arfi"y'ar hamshak'in me kud'i ce kuma y'ar gata agun iyayenta.

Tunda sukayi aure da Dr Hakeem"yafita gun aikinsa itama tafita nata gun aikin"a campany d'in mahaifita na buga sark'ok'i da y'an kunne da zobuna da saurensu...idan tafice 9am sai 6pm ko 5pm take dawowa"aranar week end bata fita"sbd tana zama ta huta agida"idan za'a mata kitso ko lalle amata.

Bishira batada lokacin mijinta"idan kinga taje gunsa kud'ine zata amsa"

Gaba d'aya bata iya girkiba"balle tsaya tagyara sashensa"saidai tasaka masu aiki"shikuwa yafad'a Mata indai bazata iya gyarawa da kantaba to tabar Masa kayansa..

Hakan yasa tabar masan kuwa"

Zuhura nason gayu da fafa"hakan yasa atsarinta batason haihuwa"Kai ita ko mutane bataso su rab'esu"dg ita sai danginta da k'awayenta"tanashan k'wayoyin hana d'aukar ciki"amma Dr Hakeem besaniba.azatonsa Allah ne bebasuba.sannan harkar auratayya"bawani kulawa take bashi ba"zai iya fita 9am yadawo 6:30 pm sai yyi magrib da isha'i yake shigowa agajiye.wanka kawai zaiyi yad'ansha coffee ya kwanta"wani time d'in ma ana iya masa kiran gaggawa a asibiti.karshe sai a week end yake samun kansa"idan kuwa ya ajiye miskilanci yazowa da zuhura da buk'atar sa"zqta sakamishi kukan kissa"ita tagaji bata lfy ko period takeyi da saurensu.hakan zai basar yahak'ura"zai iya yin 5 month beyi sex da itaba.ita kuwa zuhura jarababbiyace lamba d'aya"saidai yanayin aikinsa ba kowane lokaci yake agidaba"sannan atsarinsa yyi 3 days ko fima kafin yayi sex"sannan Dr Hakeem ba k'aramin namiji bane duk lokacin da zaiyi sex da ita zai iya yin awa 2 kofima"sannan saitayi jinyar kanta bayan yagama"hakan yasa dayazo da buk'atar sa zata yita janyewa"ita kuwa idan san samuntane kullum ayi sex. amma kar awuce 30 minit sbd tanada saurin kawowa....saidai kuma tsarinta daban nashi daban...saidai ita kullum y'ar kallon blue firm ce"(wa'iya zubillah)

Idan kuwa yakasa jurewa"to tana ihu da komai zaiyi sex da ita.

Zuhura dukda bata bashi wata kulawa tanada kishi natashin hankali.

sai akayi sa'a shi matan ma basu gabansa"ko dubaki zaiyi yaga kina wata yatsina da kisisina zai fasa yahad'a ki da Dr junud(abokinsa ne shima)

Dr Hakeem beda wasu abokan dasuka wuce Dr junud da mus'ab"mus'ab beda mata"Yana aiki agidan TV"

Wannan kenan zuwa gaba zakuji wasu abubuwan gameda zuhura..


B'angaren baby kuwa...✍️

🅿�?5&6

..... b'angaren baby kuwa cikin tsananin b'acin rai take driving d'in"inbacin tsaran ubangiji datayi accident"kalmar KARUWA!ta tsaya aranta"wani irin tsana da k'insa taji aranta"tarasa meyasa wasu kemata irin wannan ZARGI? "sosai kalaman suka tsaya Mata arai..tana isowa gida tayi parking"bayan tasaka hijab nata ta fito dg motar ta nufi cikin gidan.
Yaya bilal na mik'e akan kujera sbd gajiyar daya kwaso a office"ya amsa sallamar ta yana kallon ta.fuskarsa asake yace".autar ummi ashe dai kin musulinta?hakan yafi dacewa dake kidena shigar banzar da kikeyi..jikinsa ta fad'a tasaki kukan bak'in ciki"


       subahanallahi !ke lfy ? yafad'a yana d'ago kanta"tana kuka tace".yaya wani mugu d'an iska yadakeni"sbd yabugamun mota har gilas d'in fitilar gaba yamutu...kinsanshine?ah ah "amma zan iya ganesa idan naganshi"okay yanzun dai inama yadakeki kuma meyasa yadakekin??"fitowa nayi namasa mgn shine yazageni"saiki rama ko?"Eh"hmmm!as from to day karki koma fad'a da namiji"meya hana dayamiki b'arnar kibarshi da Allah?to amma yaya saiya kama mun masifa kamar wani Yayana....wai mitar mena kejine haka?"cewar ummi data fito tana k'ok'arin zama kan kujera.

Yaya bilal yace".autace gatanan tayi jan fad'a aka daketa"

Subahanallahi! Allah yakyauta to"amma da itada wa sukayi fad'an?"

   Labarta mata yyi"ummi tace". Allah yashiryaku keda abokin fad'an naki"amma karki koma fad'a da d'a namiji kina y'a mace..yaya bilal yace"ameen yana kallon baby yace"malama sarkin son jiki d'agani dallah...wanda yasake zuwa inda kake shine zaka disga ai ko...tana fad'in hakan ta tashi ta wuce d'akin ta"

Ummi tace".maimakon ka lallasheta saika koma tunzurata"haba kibarni da ita zanyi maganinta"yana fad'in hakan yatashi yafice sbd anata kiran sallar magrib...

Baby kuwa sallar tayi"amma abun nan daya faru nata nuk'urk'usarta..

Agajiye Dr Hakeem yakoma gidansa bayan angama sallar isha'i"

zuhura na hakimce akan kujera tana waya.

ya shigo da sallama"saurin kashe wayar tayi tana cewa sweet heart yanaji shiru pls?"wani kallo yamata batare daya tankataba yayi hanyar sashensa...

 yarasa meyasa son kud'i irin na zuhura yyi mata yawa"ita ba dg gidan matsiyata ta fitoba"amma son kud'inta yawuce tunani..bud'e k'ofar parlourn sa yayi yashiga"wani mugun tsaki yasaki"sakamakon ganin parlourn bbu gyara.sbd ak'allah parlourn zaiyi 5 days rabon da agyarashi"da week end ne idan yana gida yake gyarawa...bed room d'in yawuce shimadai bbu gyara"garama toilet da safe kafin yafita ya wanke.tsaye yyi gaban mirror yasoma rage kayan jikinsa"yana tunanin mugun son jiki irin na zuhura"ko d'azun saida yace".tagyara mishi d'akinsa amma bata gyaraba...sallamarta ta katse mishi tunaninsa"rungume sa tayi ta baya tasaki kukan kissa"musammun datasan beson yaga kukanta..shikuwa wata kasala yaji"cikin cool voice yace". menene? sweet heart kanafa jina Ina mgn kamun banza"kuma kasan next week ake bikin ko?? shiru kawai yayi sbd tsabar miskilancin sa"yanason yamata mgn amma yanajin wahalar yin maganar"zuhura sau nawa zance kidena saka gashin doki?(attached)

    miye aibunsa pls?nidai ba wannan ba ah aa muyi mgn akan kud'in fitar biki... tafad'a tana sakin kukan shagwab'a...idanuwansa alumshe yace"anjima zanturo miki"yana fad'in hakan yajuyo da ita gabansa suka fad'a kan bed....gaban zuhura yafad'i,pls nifa wlh nagaji...banza yamata yana k'ok'arin cire mata rigar baccinta"amma pls romance kawai sweet heart"uffan bece mataba yashiga sarrafata da mantar da ita duniyar datake...yakai 30 minit yana romancing d'in ta"dandanan tasamu nutsuwa. yyinda shikuma asannan yake abuk'ace"kasancewarsa gwanine Gurin iya sarrafa y'a mace..sosai zuhura keson k'watar kanta ta gudu amma yak'i bata damar hakan...ihu ,kuka ,cizo,yagi babu wanda zuhura bata mishiba"saida yakai kusan 2 hours tukum yabarta...gaba d'aya kuka takeyi takasa tashi"tamkar a daren farko haka tadingajin azaba da rad'ad'i"bayan yasuturta jikinsa"yace "sorry ya Isa haka"yi shiru kinjiko bazan koma ba...banza ta mishi yanufi ward rope d'in sa yabud'e yafito da rafar y'an 1k guda 2 ya ajiye mata kusa da ita...bece komaiba ya wuce toilet"

Tab'e baki tayi had'e da cewa 200k sune kud'in fitar bikin?

Tana d'ingishi da yarfe hannunta ta d'auki kud'in ta fice tana tafiya a wawware.

shikuwa mamaki yakeyi sosai"amatsayinsa na doctor yasan idan akayi 5 month kofi ba'a kusanci maceba "zata matse kuma itama zataso mu'amala da namiji amma shi gudunsa matakeyi...da wannan tunanin yafito yashirya ya kwanta" bacci ya k'auracewa idanunsa.sbd abinda k'aruwar nan tamishi"(harga Allah azatonsa karuwa ce"Koda bata kama d'aki ba"to karuwar gidace.ayadda yaga tayi shigar data fito Mata da suran jiki"yad'auka kanta take tallah)

*wawaaaa!!inhar kacika d'an halak ba shegeba kazubar mun da hak'oran mana*
sosai wa'annan kalaman ke amsa kuwa a kunnansa"sosai suka tsaya mishi arai"yaja tsaki yafi a k'irga...wayarsa tayi ringing"wani tsakin yakomaja kafin yad'auki wayar"junuid inhar kirana akeyi a asibiti bazan zoba gsky"hmmm!baka canka daidaiba"yanzun dai Allah yakaimu goben lfy.yana fad'in hakan yakashe wayar"maganar junuid ta biyu dayace" ko yana jikin madam sun kwanta yatsaya mishi arai...yarasa meke damun zuhura?rabon dasu kwanta a shimfid'a d'aya anfi shekara"saidai ta kwana ad'akin ta,shima yakwana anasa.idan tanayin wani abu saiyaga kamar wata bagidajiyar dabata lek'a ajiba....da wannan tunanin bacci yyi awon gaba dashi.

*bayan 7 days*

Baby har tafara zuwa aikinta cikin nassara"gefe guda kuma abinda Dr Hakeem yamata yana aranta har yanxun..dukda batasan koshi wayeba balle sunansa.fuskarsa kawai tasani...

"Eh bashi bane?"

Ammi taja ajiyar zuciya tace".nikaina wlh abun nan nadamuna sosai"yanzun ko abinci fa batayi mishi anan yake break fast da lunch"

Nafeesa tace".wlh kawai ammi asakashi yak'ara aure...anty Fatima tace".maganar kenan ai...kafin suce  wani abu ummi da baby da Usman sunshigo cikin parlourn...yyinda baby ketajin fad'uwar gaban dabatasan kona menene ba.

Ammi na murmushitace".maraba daku sannunku da zuwa!gsky yau inada babbar bak'uwa"ummi na murmushi tace".aidai nafiki son zumuncin tunda bakije munba muda ke bak'i,amma aigashi mu munzo...ammi tana dariya tace"ba haka bane hjy ,kojiya alh munyi maganar dashi wlh.

zama sukayi suka gaisa"ammi nata kallon baby data gaisheta amutunce tana sanye da hijab har k'asa"da ummi ta tursasata tasaka.suma su anty Fatima da nafeesa nata kallonta.

Ammi tace"kaddai ace SALMA ce ta girma haka?

Ummi na murmushi tace".aikuwa i tace"rannan ai Abdul Hakeem yaje yagaishemu"

Haka yake fad'a mun wlh"

salma ya gida?kan baby ak'asa tace"lfy qlau"anty Fatima tace"aimu duk bamusan junaba...ummi tace ke baby matsa gurinsu ku gaisa"tana murmushi ta tashi Takoma kusada nafisa tazauna.anan kuwa suka Fara fira har sukayi musayar contact d'in juna.suna ahaka Faisal yashigo yadawo dg ball"anan shima suka zama friend shida Usman...

Ummi taso su wuce gd" amma ammi tace" sai sunyi magrib Abba yadawo.

Duk suna atsaye suna sallama bayan sun gama sallar magrib bbu yadda ammi batayiba dasu tsaya suyi dinner sukace sunkoshi.suna ahakan Abba yashigo dawowarsa dg masjeed kenan"yakama hannun Usman duk suka gaisa cikin dad'in yaga family d'in amininsa agidansa.kallon baby yyi yace".mamana ya aikin Ina fatan Babu wata matsala ko??kasancewar shine yanema Mata aikin"tana murmushi tace". alhmdllh Abba bbu komai..Masha Allah nasan Kuna had'uwa da yayanki Abdul Hakeem acan ko?kafin tayi mgn sukaji sallamar dr Hakeem.. 

Ahankali yayi sallama"yana sanye da jallabiya brown ajikinsa"gashin kansa nata walk'iya sbd gyaran dayake sha"yayi kyau naban mamaki....tamkar yakoma haka yaji sbd arba da yyi da baby"wacce da ita yafara had'a ido..takuwa murgud'a mishi Baki...hakan yafaru akan idon Abba da anty Fatima"murmushi tayi sbd azatonta kodai akwai wani abu atsakaninsu ,dama ita tunda taga baby taji tana yiwa yayansu sha'awar aurenta"janye idanunsa yyi daga kan baby yarussuna yagaida ummi.ta amsa amutunce"

Baby kuwa inbacin darajar mutanan dake parlourn dako kallo bai ishetaba"yaya Ina wuni ? d'ago kansa yyi fuska agimtse yace"lfy qlau atakaice.su ummi suka fice ammi namusu godiya"bayan sunyi sallama da abba..Faisal nacewa Usman saiyazo gunsa.

Dr Hakeem  Kallon anty Fatima yyi had'e da cewa" me kikeyi baki tafi gida ba?"

Ayya Yaya daddynsu sadeeq zaizo ya d'aukemu ne..okay yafad'a yana wucewa dining area"tabisa da kallon tausayi...


Ammi kuwa Abba tabi asashensa...✍️

wannan shafin na y'an team d'in Dr Hakeem ne🤣🤪


🅿�?11&12

...yana zaune gefen bed yana rage kayan jikinsa ammi  ta shigo da sallama.kallonta yyi yyi yana murmushi yace".dg ganinki akwai mgn abakin nan naki ko?tana d'an murmushi ta zauna agefensa ahankali tace" nikuwa alh karkace nayi azarb'ab'i da yawa dasai nace"ka had'a aure tsakanin Abdul Hakeem da yarinyar nan baby.kuma zaku k'ara zumunci sosai da amininka....kamar kuwa kinsan hakan na ayyana araina"kuma nalura d'azun suna kallon juna"da alama kobasu son juna to sund'anyi sabo"ammi tace".hakane amma matsalar Allah yasa ya amince"amma wlh irin matarsa ba matar data dace azauna da ita itakad'ai bace...hmmm!ya Isa ince yayi yak'iyine?"cewar Abba yana kallonta.ah ah alh kawai dai nasan shi da fitina,kar ace baya sonta yaje ya musgunawa y'ar mutane muzo muji kunya....

"Abba yace".hakane kuma"

Amma zanfara yiwa alh buhari mgn tukum naji ta bakinsa.amma nikaina banaso ace da aurensa yanazuwa gidan nan yaci abinci...hakane kam bbu dad'i"Allah ya kyauta cewar Abba yana nufar toilet"

Anty Fatima naganin ammi tabar parlourn ta kalli nafeesa tace"Allah yasa yaya son yarinyar nan yakeyi.dan wlh sunyi mugun dacewa da juna"nafeesa na dariya tace"ameen nasan inya aureta zasu zuba ruwan love.dan irinsu yaya miskilai ance sunfi iya soyayya....hhhhhhhhhhh kema ai idan kinyi auren saikizo kizuba naki ruwan love d'in ko?"anty Fatima ta fad'a tana dariya had'e da kallon Dr Hakeem dake k'ok'arin wucesu yafita sbd yagama cin abincin.

Fuskarsa agimtse bbu wata walwala yace"ke tashi muje nakaiki gidanki,idan kintashi ki kira muhseen d'in basai yazoba.(mijinta) sannan karki koma kai magrib duk inda zakije"dato ta amsa tana k'ok'arin tashi ammi tashigo parlourn anan sukayi sallama taja yaran tabishi gurin motarsa.

bayan yamik'a su anty Fatima gida, yatsaya yayi sallar isha'i kafin ya wuce nashi gidan....saidai yayi mamakin ganin k'ofar waiting parlourn arufe"alamar zuhura bata dawoba dg gurin k'unshin bikin  k'anwarta (kunshi day)....wani b'acin rai yaji"yasha fad'a mata tadena yin dare idan tafita amma bata fasawa.

Key yasaka ya bud'e k'ofar yashiga"wayarsa yazaro yaga 8:30 na dare"kiranta yyi yaji wayarta akashe....

Part nasa ya wuce yaje yayi wanka yyi shirin kwanciya bacci"alokacin har 9:pm tayi"sake komawa yyi awaiting parlour yaga har yanzun batadawo ba"

zama yyi gefen kujera yana huci....adaidai nan zuhura ta murd'o k'ofar ta shigo"gabanta taji yafad'i sbd yadda taga k'wayar idanunsa.duk iskancinta idan ransa na ab'ace tana tsoronsa.....dg Ina kike?muryarsa tadaki dodon kunnanta"yafad'a yana kallon ta bbu alamar wasa afuskarsa... I'm sorry sweetheart kasan yanayin biki"gobe Inaso dg gurin dinner na kwana agida.sbd kasan za'a yi dare....wani mugun tsaki yadoka had'e da cewa"narasa meyasa bakyajin mgn ta? kinfi son b'acin raina akan komai ko?bafa hak...yimun shiru"sbd kinga nasakeki sakaka kina yadda kikeso "shiyasa kikeyin hakan ko?to barikiji bazai yuyuba wlh"d'azun kafin kifita y'ar uwata jinina tazo gidan nan kin nuna halin ko inkula da ita da yaranta.hakan daidai ne?shiyasa gaba d'aya dangina kecewa bakida kirki.to barikiji wlh inason family nah"bazan lamunci amusguna musuba.sbd haka dole kikiyaye inhar kinason zaman lfy dani agidan nan...maganar zuwa dinner gobe nadatseta bbu ita"

What's?"kasan kuwa mekake fad'a kuwa?

bansaniba"yafad'a cikin dakakkiyar Murya"had'e rai tayi tana Ida k'arasowa cikin parlourn"cikin tsiwa tace".okay yanzun nagane kaje anzugoka shine ka b'ulle da hakan?to wlh bbu wanda ya Isa yahanani zuwa gurin hidimar bikin  k'anwata"kamar yadda kakeson family naka nima haka nakeson nawa nima...yana huci ya mik'e tsaye yana tunkarota"had'e da cewa maimaitamun abinda kika fad'a...kasancewarta y'ar kissa"saima tasaki kukan shagwab'a tafad'a jikinsa ta mak'alk'alesa ta rushe da kuka...tsaki yaja had'e da cewa kicikani malama"haba sweet heart!pls I'm really very sorry"tafad'a tana kukan kissa"banza yamata yafara k'ok'arin janyeta dg jikinsa...haba mana sweet heart nace bazan komaba ko?yakamata ka hak'ura dan ALLAH!kicikani nace ko"sakinsa tayi dama tana tsoron kar yace zai kwad'a mata mari...
Tsaki yaja yayi hanyar sashensa, ta tab'e baki ta wuce bed room d'in ta"tana k'ok'arin kiran wata number...

Dr Hakeem kuwa ransa bbu dad'i yawuce sashensa"yakumasha alwashin baza tajeba.idan beyi da gaskeba zuhura na neman tafi k'arfin sa, sbd ba wannan ne karo na farko daya hanata fita tak'ijiba"sau tari yana sakata har aiki tace".tagaji bakuma zatayi d'in ba.atsarinsa besan zama da mace 2"yafiso ta gyara wasu abubuwan suzauna lfy"shi mutum ne mai sauk'i akan iyalinsa.yanzun haka kuku yakeso yad'auka yadinga musu girki yana biyansa albashi"tunda ammi na fad'an yana zuwa yanacin abinci acan gashi yanada mata....wasu abubuwan da yawa yana b'oyewa ammi ne su gameda zamansa da zuhura. yasan wlh  da tuni iyayensa sun d'auki mataki.dandai kawai yana boyewane besan su tsaneta...

da wannan tunanin ya kira wayarta yajita busy...sosai yyi mamakin k'arfe 9:30 dawa zatayi waya...tana cikin wayar taga kiransa"hakan yasa ta dakata da wayar ta kirashi"tamkar bazai d'auka ba.yadai daure yad'aga...sweet heart Ina waya da Malika (amaryar)nayi mantuwa agida naga kiranka..saida yagama shan k'amshin sa tukum yace"nabaki nan da 10 minit kizo d'akina mu kwanta...harta cire rai dazaiyi mgn taji kalamansa.

shagwab'e fuska tayi tana kukan kissa tace"nidai kamun alk'awari bazakamun komaiba.kasan gajaniyar biki... yana jan tsaki"yace bana buk'atar zuwanki kiyi zamanki...yana fad'in hakan yakashe wayar yayi kwanciyarsa bayan yasakawa d'akin key.

*washe gari*

Zuhura kuwa ko ajikinta mijinta na fushi da ita"bayan ta tashi tayi salar asuba da k'arfe 7:30 am ta wuce part nasa..

Yana kwance kan bed yakoma bacci kafin 8 tayi ,yyi shirin xuwa office...haurowa tayi kan bed d'in tahaye saman jikinsa tana jijjigasa"haushi sosai tabashi"sbd yatsani yana bacci atasheshi...Amma jin tana mishi shagwab'a saiyakasa bud'e ido balle yace"mata wani abu...shikansa yasan shagwab'a nasaurin sakashi yarasa nutsuwarsa"shiyasa yakeso tadinga mishi"amma batayi inba wani abu takeso ba agunsa"shinefa zatahaye masa jiki tana kukan kissa da shagwab'a....

ganin tak'i ta dena"gashi yasan idan tazo hannusa zasu iya kai 10 am  berabu da itaba.hakan yasakashi cewa menene?

nidai kayi hakuri kabarni naje bazan kai dareba...tashi kije....berufe bakinsaba tabar jikin sa ta fice da sauri.

ya girgiza kansa yatashi ya wuce bathroom.

****************
Baby da Dr Hakeem suna yawan had'uwa a seminars and work shops"amma basa kula juna"

wani lokacin da gangan Dr Hakeem zaiyi wani abunda dole yasakata aiki"hakan yak'ara mata tsanarsa.. doctors da nurses mata suyita sintirin xuwa gaisheshi"amma ita saidai tabisa da harara.shikuwa sbd kawai yamatsa mata saiyadinga yawan zuwa asibitin..

Yau sunje wu
rin wani work shop akan new drugs da akayi producing na hawan jini.

baby stress yamata yawa batayi ko break fast ba bare cin abinci har yamma..

suna fitowa kamar da wasa tafara ganin jiri jiri...saidai kawai sukaga ta zube ak'asa"

Dr KB na k'ok'arin kamata da wata nurse"Dr Hakeem yyi saurin dakatar dashi da cewa mlm karka tab'ata....gaba d'aya sunyi mamaki mutanan dake gurin,sbd sunsan basa shiri.

D'aukarta akayi aka shiga da ita ciki"MD yace"Dr Hakeem yadubata.

Yatsaya akanta yamata dubarunsu irin na likitoci"amma shiru bata farkaba"gashi marece yayi"Dr KB yana dg waje yanajiran farkawarta.sbd har yanxun sonta yakeyi"

wasu dg cikin nurses sunyi mamakin yadda yatsaya yabata kulawa sbd sunsan bedamuwa da kowa.

Sai gurin 6:30 pm ta farka"bbu kowa d'akin sai sister larai.aikuwa tashiga bata labarin yadda yabata kulawa"

Tab'e baki tayi tana k'ok'arin tashi"Dr Hakeem yashigo d'akin"hakan yasa sister larai saurin ficewa.

Kallonta yyi suka had'a ido...✍️

🅿�?13&14

Janye idanunsa yyi dg barin kallonta" yana yatsina fuska tamkar yaga kashi ko wani abu na k'yama...itakuwa tab'e baki tayi tana k'ok'arin tashi ahankali tace"thanks!banza yamata yana danna waya cikin izzah! yad'auki kusan second 6 kafin yace".it is my duty"sbd haka bana buk'atar godiyarki....inkin gadama kirik'a cin abinci akan lokaci"stress ne yamaki yawa...idan banga dama ba fa? tafad'a cikin tsiwa"da sauri yad'ago kansa yace".dani kike?dama muna wane acikin d'akin? tafad'a tana k'ok'arin tafiya....saidai kawai tagansa agabanta"gabanta yafad'i!saidai bata nunu alamar tsorata ba.tayi saurin yin baya..bakida kunya bakida girmama mutane ko?"ko dayake banyi mamakin hakan ba ,sbd ke k'azamiyace... yafad'a yana d'age kafad'a"alamar ko ajikinsa"ban da ita saika koyamun..ZARGINA dakakeyi nabarka da Allah!da sauri yamatsa yakai hannunsa gefen kunnanta yamurd'e da k'arfi"zaki koma yimun rashin kunya?sa'ankine nine?
     tamakar zata saki fitsari sbd azaba.....tasaki k'ara had'e da cewa kacikamun kunnena...zaki sake mun rashin kunya?wani azaba taji...aikuwa tayi saurin chafkar hannunsa guda ta gartsa mishi cizo"hakan yasa yyi saurin sakin kunnanta... tayi saurin jada baya tana gallamishi harara tace".wlh duk ranar daka cika na 3. saina maka abinda bakayi zatoba"akanme zaka kai hannunka ajikina?last time kamareni ,yanzun ka murd'an kunne...to wlh baza kacika na 3 ba"tunda tacijesa yake kallon hannunsa harta batamishi gefen hannun riga da lipstick nata...kasancewar suit farace yasaka me dogon hannu"bece mata komaiba sbd b'acin rai"haushinta da tsanar ta yaji aransa...yajuya yafice dg d'akin"itama fitowa tayi duk da k'asan ranta taga beci ace tamishi rashin kunya ba.kodan sbd kulawar dayabata...da wannan tunanin ta fito Dr KB yabiyota"wani mugun disgi tamishi tayi wucewarta bakin hanya ta hau adaidaita sahu...

Dr Hakeem kuwa asibitin sa yawuce acan yyi sallar magrib,kafin yadawo gida"

babu kowa a waiting parlourn"hakan yasa yawuce sashensa"zuhura na bed room d'inta ta fito ta wuce d'akin sa sbd ganin kiransa datayi ayanzun.

yana tsaye gaban bed yana k'ok'ari cire suit d'in jikinsa ta shigo...kamar ance takalli hannunsa taga janbaki"zaro ido tayi cikin fad'uwar gaba da zafin kishi tace".menake gani a hannun rigarka kamar lipstick? tafad'a tana nuna mishi had'e da tunkarosa....lumshe ido yyi aransa yace".fitina kenan!sbd yatuna lipstick d'in baby ne data cijeshi ad'azun yagoga.kanajinafa kayi shiru alamar bakada gsky kenan? tafad'a tana rushewa da kuka..cikin tsantsar tashin hankali da damuwa tace"kayi mgn, kuma gsky zaka fad'a mun tun kafin zuciyata ta buga....

Kinga yishiru karkimun wani ZARGI daban"

wata patient ce aka kawo emergency"shine gurin bata treatment na gogi lip stick nata...wlh banyar daba!karka nemi kamayardani  wata shashasha Mana"auren likita ko matsala!

ada tabbas na yarda dakai amma yanzun Ina shakku...wato masu cewa suna sankane ko?

wlh bazai yuyuba koma wacece ita.amana ta zakaci? kaje kayi kissing nat....shout up !bakida hankaline?kishin banza zaisakaki kimun kazafi ko mummunan zato"to bari kiji idan yanda kike zato ayanzun haka nike" da wlh bazakiyi zaman sati guda agidan nan.

Miye amfanin kishin nakine?okay kince nayi kissing wata ko?ke yaushe rabonki daki bani naki bakin nayi kissing?shiru kawai tayi tana share hawayenta....yaja tsaki had'e da cewa dani mutumin banzane  wlh bazama ki ganni agidanba,balle kiyimun wannan haukan...cikin fad'a tace".hakama zakace?"

bansaniba!yana fad'in hakan yajuya ya wuce bath room.

sosai zuhura taci burin yin bincike a asibitin inma k'arya yamata to wlh zataga bayan kowacece ita.indai boka da malam na raye"

Tajima tana kuka kafin ta tashi tayi tafiyarta.

Dr Hakeem kuwa bayan yafito yaso yaje ya rarrasheta"amma sbd tunawa da yayi idan yayi fushi da ita bata wani damuwa ko rarrashinsa saima ta share shi kawai"hakan yasa yabarta.

bayan yagama shiryawa yasaka jallabiya yafito sbd zaije masjeed yyi isha'i.


Yana ahanya Abba yakirashi idan angama isha'i yazo yanasan mgn dashi...

******************

Baby kuwa jikinta bbu k'arfi ta Isa gida"sosai taci ta k'oshi"tayi wanka da sallar magrib"

Ummi tafad'a mata kartayi bacci akwai wata mgn da abbansu zaiyi da ita idan yadawo masjeed..

haka nan  baby tasami kanta dajin fad'uwar gaba"tadai daure ta amsa da to"


saidai abinda baby bata saniba Abba da abbansu Dr Hakeem sun zauna sunyi mgn"ranar al'hamis magabatan Dr Hakeem sunje kurfi nema masa aurenta...nan da wata 2 akasaka"yaya bilal  da nafeesa sai itada     Dr Hakeem...

Akuma yau 3 days kenan da saka bikin..

Bayan Abba yadawo dg masjeed yaci abinci, yasaka ummi takira mishi baby bayan yakira Yaya Bilal awaya yazo.

Yaya Bilal yarigata zuwa sbd dama yana a sashensa Abba yakirashi.

Ahankali tashigo parlourn gabanta na fad'uwa! musammun dataga yaya bilal azaune...

mamana zoki zauna anan"Abba yafad'a yana nuna mata kusa dashi...

gyaran murya Abba yayi bayan ta zauna"ahankali yace". alhmdllh Bilal najima Ina maka maganar kayi aure amma saikace baka fitarba...gaban Yaya Bilal yafad'i!yana k'ok'arin mgn Abba yace"amatsayina n a mahaifinka nazab'ama nafeesa y'ar aminina amtsayin matar aurenka nan da wata 2 insha ALLAH...jikin yaya bilal asanyaye yace". Allah yakaimu Abba"saidai aransa besan ko waceceba"sbd betab'a ganinta ba"sannan yasan itama bata sonshi tunda had'a auren akayi.

maida kallonsa Abba yayi ga baby,wacce gabanta yatsananta fad'uwa sbd jin nafeesar gidansu Dr Hakeem yaya Bilal zai aura.mamana kema dai hakan yake anaki b'angaren.

 Kinga karatu harkin fara aiki amma kink'i fito da miji kiyi aure....

Sbd haka nabawa abdul Hakeem aurenki nanda wata 2 atare za'a yi bikinku gaba d'aya insha Allah....zaro idanuwa tayi cikin tashin hankali da fargaba tace".Abba ni wlh bana shonshi !ko waye kahad'ani dashi amma banda yaya Hakeem... tafad'a tana fashewa da kuka!Kinga mamana kimun shiru"ko kukan jini zakiyi aure atsakaninku Babu fashi.munriga mun gama mgn da alh nuhu ansaka rana"dama kuma ba amincewarku mukeda buk'ataba.ah ah munsanar muku ne sbd kar abin yazo muku abazata...sannan muddin kikace kibujirewa umarnina to saidai kinemi wani uban baniba...yana fad'in hakan yatashi tsaye yabar parlourn"

Fashe wa da kuka takomayi tafad'a jikin Yaya Bilal.

Jikinsa asanyaye yace"kinutsu muyi mgn.yazata aransa zata amince da auren kodan sbd Dr Hakeem akwai farin jini da kamala"

     Ahankali yace"kiyi hakuri muyiwa Abba biyayya mana"hmmm!Yaya kenan aikai kadace ,kuma kayi sa'a wlh"sbd nafeesa tayi zakayi farin ciki da aurenta insha Allah....nikuwa nasan zanshiga bak'in ciki da damuwa da b'acin rai.
     Duk akan me?yatambayeta yana kallon ta"Yaya natsanesa baya sona,bana sonshi" shinefa ko yau saida yamur...banasonjin komai inhar kikayi hakuri kikayi biyayya ga iyayenki zakiga riba arayuwarki"kije kiyita addu'ar zab'in alkhairi.yafada yana sakinta"batace komai ba ta tashi ta wuce d'akin ta...

Ya lumshe ido yana tunanin ko yaya nafeesar take itakuma...

Dr Hakeem kuwa ana gama isha'i yashigo gida"yasami Abba da ammi a parlour.sannu da gida yamusu"ammi ta tashi ta kira nafeesa"

Abba yadubesa yace"dakai zan fara Abdul Hakeem"nagaji damun zarya dakakeyi agida bacin ganaka gidan.kuma kanada iyali"saidai abinda nafahimta iyalin naka bata iya kulawa dakai ko?

    Kansa ak'asa bece komaiba"dukda aransa beso ana masa mgn agaban nafeesa ba"

Abba yace"to dakai da nafeesa nan da wata 2 aurenku da muka saka nida alh buhari ... Nafeesa zata auri bilal"kaikuma baby SALMA.....✍️

Bansaniba"

    Ahankali yace."karki damu babu komai saiki bawa Faisal taki tsohuwar....batasan lokacin data shagwab'e fuskaba tace".yaron nan yarainani ban Isa na aikesaba....nibazan bashiba"lumshe ido yyi sbd jin tayi mugun burgesa! sbd yanason mace me shagwab'a"murya can k'asa yace".kici girma my Queen kibarmasa kinjiko?itama baby haka suke yi da usman ayi fad'a ashirya.tsakanin mabiyi da mabiyi haka suke ai"shikenan yaya bilal zanbashi to"yauwa my queen ,ni yanzun zanwuce ankusa magrib"sakamun number d'in naki zankiraki da dare"yafad'a yana k'ok'arin mik'a mata wayar.

bayan tasaka mishi ya karb'a yana mik'ewa tsaye"yace"zanwuce  muje kirakani...ahankali itama ta tashi suka jero suka fito"yanajanta da fira har suka iso gurin motarsa... adedenan kuma Abba yafito dg motar sa...sosai yaji dad'in ganinsu ahakan.har k'asa yaya Bilal yaduk'a yagaidashi"yyinda nafeesa tamishi sannu da zuwa....

bayan Abba ya wuce yaya bilal yakalleta yana murmushi yace".zanwuce yazan cewa ummi?kanta ak'asa tace".Ina gaidata"sosai ko kad'an? yafad'a cikin wata iriyar murya"shagwab'e fuska tayi had'e da cewa nidai sosai"shikenan natafi saina kiraki da daren ko?      Da dare kuwa sunjima Babu laifi suna waya"tun nafeesa na nok'ewa bata sakin jikinta harta dai saki jiki...

Masha Allah a b'angaren 2 duk shirye shirye akeyi babu kama hannun yaro"ankai lefen nafeesa akwati12"lefen baby 24"masha Allah abin sai son barka"komai yayi sai hamdala... yayinda anty Fatima sune k'irjin biki...

zaune suke a parlourn bak'i shida Dr junuid daya tursasashi suzo abawa friends nata wani abu na hidimar biki...

Tsaki Dr Hakeem yadoga akaro na bbu adadi"yana kallon agogon dake daure a tsintsiyar hannunsa yace".kaga abinda nake fad'a maka ko?

Dr junuid na k'ok'arin mgn aka turo k'ofar...✍️

zuhura na zaune tare da Malika (k'anwarta da akayi bikinta kwanaki)dasu sholy da feedy manyan k'awayenta sunata bata muyagun shawarwari....har yashigo basu saniba" gabansu lemummukane da kaji soyayyu gandambun shinkafa yaji coslow...duk sunci sunyi hani'an...ai wlh k'awata karki bari yakusanci y'ar iska"idan tamiki gargada ki jibki shegiya....idan nazo natayaki wlh"cewar Malika suna sakin shewa.....k'amshin turaren sa yagauraye parlourn" hakan yasaka antar cikin zuhura kad'awa"da sauri takalli k'ofar shigowa....yana.tsaye fuskarsa bbu alamar rahama acikinta !yana binsu da wani irin mugun kallo"idanuwansa sund'anyi jaa..yana mamakin halin xuhura,indai wani nata zaizo gidan haka zata bashi cima kalakala"amma shida yake nemowa danginsa idan sunxo ko ruwa ba'a basu ,saidai idan yyi mgn.za'a basu bacikin dad'in rai ba... sweet heart! zuhura tayi saurin anbata tana tashi tsaye"bece komaiba ya haye upstairs"da sauri ta rufa mishi baya...

   tsaki yaja had'e da cewa bansaniba!kibari nafito nasameta wlh atare dake zankoreku"idan suntafi kizo inason mgn dake...gabanta nafad'uwa ta juya ta fice...

     d'ago kansa yyi cikin muryarsa me bawa marar jin mgn tsoro yace".naji rashin mutuncin da kikayiwa dangina dana  amaryata....sosai *amaryata* tadaki zuciyarta"tana k'ok'arin mgn yace" inaso kisani gobe insha Allah za'a kawota agidan nan"matsayinku d'aya da ita"yadda na aureki itama haka"bazan lamunci tamiki rashin kunyaba.kema haka "sannan duk ranar dakika koma yiwa wani nawa abinda baidaceba wlh zand'auki mummunan mataki akanki...magana ta k'arshe kiyiwa shed'anun k'awayen ki da y'ar uwarki magana suka koma zuwa min gida saina saka anrufemin su a cell...fashewa da kuka zuhura tayi"cikin k'unar Rai tace". wannan dalilin yasaka da yawa mata basason kishiya.tun kafin ta shigo zaka Fara wulak'antani?insu  su sholy friends d'ina ne,Malika k'anwata ce!babu kuwa wanda ya Isa yarabani da ita wlh...amaryarka dakake ikrari ko?to ai nasan komai akanta har had'uwarku ta farko da buga mata mota da kay....rufemin baki konayi k'asa k'asa dake!wawiya wacce batasan mutuncin mijin aurenbtaba.meya hana kisanar mun  shed'anin daya gayamiki"wato kinsamun ido kina bimun k'waf ko?

Shiru kawai tayi batace komai ba"sbd tasan halinsa idan yana kan dokin zuciya...tashi tsaye tayi tana kuka tace".Babu komai kayimun duk wulak'ancin daka gadama...tana fad'in hakan ta juya ta fice...

    Tsaki yaja had'e da cewa ai gobe ne ko zaki sani yarinya wlh kikashigo gidan nan..

Hakan yasa duk jikin baby yayi sanyi...

🅿�? 25&26

    washe gari.....da misalin k'arfe 11 am d'aruruwan jama'a suka shaida d'aurin auren"ABDUL HAKEEM NUHU KURFI da SALMA BUHARI KURFI.sai BILAL BUHARI KURFI da NAFEESA NUHU KURFI...sosai zuciyar Dr Hakeem tadinga bugawa lokacin da aka sanar and'aura auren"yana sanye da wani tsadaddan d'anyen bowel fari"y'ar ciki da malun malun.anmasa aiki da bak'in zare"jikinsa nafitar da wani k'amshi na musammun.yanata murmushin k'arfin hali...sbd beson mutane sugane bayason auren, musammun abban baby da abbansa...yanata kallon yaya Bilal dake sanye cikin dakakkiyar shadda milk ammata aiki da brown d'in zare.. yanata  gaisawa da jama'a"kallo d'aya zaka masa kasan yanason auren...aransa yace"wato shi yanason auren kenan?"

🅿�? 37&38

........da sauri baby ta bud'e idanuwanta arikice"wani murmushin gefen baki yasaki"wanda yyi mugun Masa kyau"kafin yace".meye abin jin tsoro? bacin abin ba bak'onki bane.....rasa mezatace mishi tayi"hakan yasa yad'agata"tayi saurin mirginawa ta  sauka dg kan bed d'in tana maida numfashin gajiya.... tab'e baki yyi had'e da d'age kafad'a,irin ko ajikinsa d'in nan"yana yatsina fuska yace". idan kin gama kukan munafurcin kitashi muje d'akina acan zanci abincin"saiki kamun a parlour.naga kina son guduwa ko?banza tayi dashi tana share hawayenta"sauka dg kan bed d'in yayi yana cewa"oya muje ko nab'ata miki rai... k'in tankasa tayi"sbd talura son tayi mgn yakeyi"binsa tayi suka fito a parlourn"

     baby kuwa key ta danna a k'ofar ta"aranta tanaja mishi Allah ya isa"

da dare misalin k'arfe 8:30"yanufi parlourn ta bayan yadawo dg masjeed"ya murd'a yaji arufe"yasaka key tak'i bud'ewa k'ofar sbd tabar key d'in ajiki...sosai ransa yab'aci"metake  nufine wai yarinyar nan? yafad'a afili yanayiwa k'ofar wani mahaukacin knocking.

    baby kuwa tana zaune tana Azkhar d'in ta"tasan  sarai shine amma ta sharesa taci gaba da abinda takeyi"kitchen nata ya wuce yaduba flux"yaga akwai ruwan zafi"coffee yahad'a ya fice....ko washe gari da safe"tana gama sallar asuba ta wuce kitchen"ta had'a break fast agaggauce sbd bataso yazo yasameta...dan yanzun wani tsoronsa ma takeji"komai ta ajiye kan dining table ta d'ibi nata ta wuce bed room d'in ta tadanna key"sbd taga har 7:30 ta wuce kuma 8:am ka'ida yake fitowa. sbd idan yagama sai yaje ya gaidasu su ammi yake wucewa office.

Ahankali ya shigo cikin parlourn"yana tafiyarsa ta isassun mazajen dasuka amsa sunansu maza"fuskarsa tamkar hadarin gabas sbd yadda take a murtuke....harara dining area d'in yayi"kafin ya wuce k'ofar bed room d'in ta yamurd'a yajita arufe"kwafa taja ya wuce dining area d'in"zuciyar sa nasanarmasa shine kebatason gani shiyasa take hakan...

🅿�? 15&16

......wani irin mugun bugawa k'irjin Dr Hakeem yyi"amma be nuna hakanba...kasancewarsa mutum ne meyiwa iyayensa biyayya"saidai yasha alwashin zaiyiwa ammi mgn dg ita saishi"amma bayajin zai amince ya auri baby ragowar titi....nafeesa ma anata b'angaren gabanta yafad'i"sbd dai a fuska batasan Yaya Bilal ba"sannan idan baya sontafa....uwa uba kuma tsoron sure takeyi...Abba yace"kunyi shiru bakuce komaiba"suma acan  haka alh buhari yasanar musu.kuyi hak'uri kuyi Mana biyayya zakuci riba agaba....cikin b'oye yanayin daya tsinci kansa akan jin wannan mummunan labarin yace".mungode Abba! Allah yasa hakan shiyafi alkhairi...ameen yah Allah Allah yayimuku albarka"tashi Dr Hakeem yyi jikinsa asanyaye yayiwa Abba sallama da nufin gobe in Allah yakaimu da safe yayiwa ammi mgn akan zancen...

Nafeesa ma kasa cewa komai tayi"gabanta nata fad'uwa ta tashi ta wuce d'akin ta.sai asannan ammi ta dawo parlourn gun Abba...

A b'angaren baby kuwa kuka tadingayi"tana jin zafin wai Dr Hakeem mak'iyin ta dako yau saida yaci zalin nata a sibiti"shine zaizama mijinta,kuma yanada mata akaita gidan kishiya....idan kishiyar marar Imani ce su had'u itadashi suyita gallaza mata....sosai ta kwanta cikin damuwa tana fatan gari ya waye idan zataje gun aiki taje gidansu raudah koda akwai shawarar dazata bata....Amma batajin zata auri wanda ke mata wulak'anci yana kiranta da karuwa.. bacin bacci barawone dabatayin  bacci sbd damuwa"

Dr Hakeem kuwa cikin tsananin damuwa ya iso gida"ko neman zuhura beyiba"sbd yasan fushi takeyi da shi..bed room nasa ya wuce yaje ya sake wanka yayi shirin kwanciya bacci.... tunanin farkon had'uwarshi da baby yayi"da ranar da yasami Dr KB a office nata yana gyara wondonsa....rintse idanunsa yyi yana huci"da k'arfi ya furta it can!nafi k'arfin narayu da ita a inwar ma'aurata" bacin tana ragowar wasu....tunawa yyi da first night d'insu da zuhura itamafa ba virgin yasametaba..sbd uncle d'in ta yayi reping nata"ya yarda da k'addara"sbd yasan ita ba mazinaciya bace...Amma baby yarinyar da mara barta da karuwa kad'an ne ban bancinsu"danma yalura acikin 2 days tarage shigar banza....yakuma fahimci tabbas iyayenta basusan metake aikatawa ba..zuciyarsa nazafi da k'unci ya lumshe ido yana tuna shikenan shi tashi jarabawar ahaka zatazo"kowane namiji yana burin ya auri y'ar k'warai"gashi inhar ya auri baby itama yasan ba virgin bace.. tsaki yaja yana ayyana aransa koya aureta bazaiyi sex da ita ba...

Tunawa yyi da abinda yafaru ad'azun tsakaninsa da zuhura"yasan muddin taji maganar tofa zata tashi hankalinta, shima tatada mishi nashi...afili yace"aiduk kece sanadin komai"inda kin kula dani aihakan bazai faruba"

yajima yana tunani har bacci yyi awon gaba dashi"

    da safe yana tsaye gaban dressing mirror yana taje suman kansa"fuskarsa bbu annuri ko kad'an,zuhura tashigo bbu ko sallama.tana k'ok'arin zama kan bed d'in sa" yad'ago kansa ya watsa mata jajayen idanuwansa"saida taji tsoro musammun da yadda taga fuskarsa agimtse....idan bazaki iya yimun sallama ba to kificemun dg d'aki"yana fad'in hakan yajanye idanuwansa yacigaba da abinda yakeyi...cikinnjin haushi tace".babu damuwa adakeni a hanani kuka"kamun laifi bazaka rarrasheni ba,nazo ni "shine kakoreni ko ?to yayi kyau"bata koma mgn ba ta fice dg d'akin.

K'ananun kaya yasaka"yayiwa jikinsa wankan turare"ywyi ksyau kamar d'an saurayi..kafin yafito besakebi takan zuhura ba yaja motar sa.yabar mata gidanma gaba d'aya...

     Dr Hakeem yakai bakinsa saitin kunnanta yace".wlh kinutsu yarinya!kikasake kikayi gulmata kinsan sauran....sajen fuskarsa ta fisga da k'arfi....hakan yasaka yacikata da sauri yana murmushin yak'e"kallon yaya Bilal yayi yace".dawowata kenan dg office nawuce d'akina"yaya Bilal yace".okay saika fito"Dr Hakeem yace".autar ammi dg zuwa saikuma....nadaiyi shiru"nafeesa naturo baki tace".saime?shareta  yayi ya wuce side nashi.sai asannan baby tarungume nafeesa tana murmushin farin ciki"tazo tafad'a jikin yaya bilal"hancinta yaja had'e da cewa" kewai bazaki dena tab'araba?hmmm!Yaya nayi fushi daku"sau d'aya katab'a kirana"kema anty nafeesa....duk idan nakira wani lokacin bana samu"kuma ke a what's app naga kinjima baki online....Yaya Bilal yace".mezatayi a online ga sabon babynta? murmushi kawai baby tayi cike da kunya ta wuce kitchen takamusu snacks da drinks...nafeesa lemo kawai tasha"sai asannan itama baby ta lura da yanayin nafeesar namasu yaron ciki ne...aranta tace".wai Allah!gsky Yaya Bilal jarababbene lamba d'aya!dg zuwa sai ciki....sosai taji sun burgeta , musammun yadda suke bawa junansu kulawa"sunfi 35minit"kafin Yaya Bilal yace".my Queen!tashi muje"b'ata fuska baby tayi had'e da cewa"dg zuwa sai tafiya yaya?yaunefa tafara fita"zamuje gidan ummi da ammi"sai muje gidan anty Fatima"kinga har 3:30 tayi"cewar Yaya Bilal"baby tace".hakane fa"to muma muna nan tafe insha Allah"to shikenan saikunzo"yaya bilal yafad'a yana k'ok'arin mik'ar da nafeesa tsaye... adedenan Dr Hakeem yashigo cikin shirinsa na k'ananun kaya dasuka yi mugun karb'an sa"badai tafiyaba? yafad'a yana kallon su...baby tace".eh wai Yaya"muma idan mukaje gidansu bazamu dad'eba ko?

Nafeesa fuskarta Babu walwala tace".anty zuhura Ina wuni?lfy!zuhura tafad'a atakaice tana wani shan k'amshi da hurahanci.tana jifar baby dawani kallo"Yaya Bilal kuwa yadda ta amsa gaisuwar nafeesa yasakashi ya d'auke kansa yyi gaba"batare daya tankataba"hakan kuwa yayiwa baby dad'i.tawani mak'ale jikin Dr Hakeem tana cewa"Yaya Bilal saimunzo muma insha Allah"anty nafeesa mungode sosai maman twins....harararta nafeesa tayi tana k'ok'arin fita"Dr Hakeem yace".yes!ai da gaskiyarta....wani mugun tsaki zuhura taja"tana taunar chiweengum...su nafeesa na fita"baby tawani langab'e ajikinsa"yaya nidai kad'aukeni wlh duk nagaji"hmmm!aikin mekikayine dana fita?malam karfa kanemi ka rainan hankali"kasan yau girkinane kake lik'e da ita"idan ita jarababbiyace kaima hakan?ransa ab'ace yake kallonta sbd dama tabashi haushi akan amsa gaisuwar data yiwa nafeesa....ai jarabar ma aji ce"kema saikiyi k'ok'ari ki koya"andaiji kunya ankori mutum sbd rashin zuciya yadawo....cewar baby tana kwantowa ajikinsa"kutumar ubancan!kanajifa?okay kaine kasanar mata dawowata kenan ko?wlh za'ayi masifa da bala'i anan gidan...kicin kicin da fuska yayi had'e da cewa Allah yarabamu dasu"inhar kikace zaki koma ma halinki wlh bazamu shirya dakeba"wayace kifara tak'alarta?"bejira cewartaba yasab'i baby sukabar parlourn.zuhura sbd bak'in ciki saita fashe da kuka ta wuce side nata...

suna shiga parlourn ta"baby tace"sakeni ni saika koma gunta"in ank'ifa?shiru tayi tana hawaye...cikin muryar kuka tace". kamanta wulak'ancin dakamun ad'azun da safe"shine kaika manta ko?to duk abinda kakemun Allah naganinka...ajiyeta yyi kan kujera"yana cewa nibanmiki komaiba"itama tasamun ciwon Kai gara kema kiyi k'ok'arin sakamun zazzab'i kinjiko?"yafad'a Yana k'ok'arin bin hanyar dining area"baby tadakatar dashi da cewa" banyi girki dakaiba.kuma bazan sake yi dakaiba"kaje can gun wacce keda girki tabaka"ita kadama hartake hura hanci sbd kai... baby nikike gayama mgn son ranki? yafad'a afusace yana nufota....da gudu tayi bed room d'in ta ta rufe...

Parlourn zuhura yakoma yasamu tana zaune"sallama yayi ahankali"kasancewar tazuba magani a abinci tanason yaci"saita b'oye haushinsa datakeji...sweet heart ga abincifa najiranka"yatsina fuska yyi had'e da cewa yaushe kikafara mun girki agidan nan?hmmm! nidai gsky banason kanamun haka"kinga nasan baki iya girkiba"nikuma bazan iya cin girkin masu aikiba"sbd haka na k'oshi...bejira cewartaba yabar parlourn"sosai zuhura tashak'i haushi da takaici taja kwafa kawai..

Shirinta tagama tsab"kafin tasaka mayafinta ta wuce parking space taja motarta tabar gidan....Dr junuid dake k'ok'arin shigowa layin da motarsa"kasancewar yau asabar yanaso yazo gidan yasami abokinsa. sbd suyi mgn da baby gameda raudah.da sauri yayi baya tawuce"kafin yayi kwana yacigaba da binta abaya...zuhura kuwa batasanma dashiba balle tasan yanabinta....

wani had'add'an guri na hutawa tayi parking ta fito ta shiga ciki.

Kusan 10 minit Dr junuid yafito shima yashiga cikin girin...

Zuhura na zaune kusada mus'ab"suna mgn tana murmushi...tana d'ago kanta suka yi ido 4 da Dr junuid"had'e rai tayi tana yiwa mus'ab alamar yakalli bayansa.yana juyowa yaga junuid yaja tsaki....Dr junuid na k'arasowa yayi murmushi batare daya zaunaba yace".ku kwantar da hankalinku batare dashi mukeba"kuma bazan sanar mishiba"insha Allah wata rana YAU DA GOBE (book nawa...inhar baki karantashiba anbarki a mugun baya) zaigani k'uru k'uru...kunsaka ansaceni sbd kumin sharri na zina!bacin niba mazinaci bane.kune mazinata  kuma fasik'ai maciya amana...tayaya kuke zaton Allah zai baku nassara akaina? Allah yakare ni dg sharrinku....kaga malam wlh koka bar nan gurin kokuwa nabaka mamaki"inbakaje kasanar mishiba baka k'aunar Allah.cewar mus'ab Afusace! murmushi Dr junuid yayi had'e da cewa bazan sanar mishi ba"nafiso yagani dakansa wata rana..yanzunma nazone sbd nanuna muku baku Isa kumin komaiba.kuma iskancinku na k'aryane.indai kun cika tantirai kuyi inda wanda yasanku zaiganku mana...yana fad'in hakan yajuya ya barsu anan yayi tafiyarsa...

Dr Hakeem kuwa ganin idan yazauna ahakan zai iya rasa ransa ko wani mugun ciwo yakamasa"kawai yad'auki key spire ya wuce d'akin baby adaddafe...bewani yi knocking ba yasaka key yabud'e....yana saka kansa ad'akin hakan yyi daidai da fitowar baby dg wanka.tana d'aure da towel guntu rabin cinyoyinta awaje....saurin jada baya tayi had'e da turo baki ta kallesa"saurin janye idanunta tayi sbd yadda taga nashi idanuwan sunyi jajir...dan Allah kafita shiryawa zanyi...bece mata komaiba yarufe k'ofar yamurza key.zaro ido tayi had'e da cewa lfy?bece komaiba ya matso kusada ita....azubure tajuya da nufin tagudu.... sulb'in tajis yajata sbd k'afafunta akwai ruwa"tasaki k'ara yayi saurin tarota tafad'a ajikinsa.kan bed ya azata yana cewa"kingama yimun rashin kunya ajiya kika gudu ko?to yanzun nakamaki.zankuma yimiki maganin rainin dake tsakaninmu..nalura sbd baki sanni a kan bed ba shiyasa kike hakan"amma zaki sanni ayau wlh...dan  girman Allah kayi hakuri nadena! tafad'a arud'e tana k'ok'arin rik'e mishi hannu sbd taga zai kwance mata towel...hannu guda yasaka yarik'e hannayeta, hannun guda yafiske mata towel nata.... k'ara tasaki sbd bbu ko pant ajikinta.dan Allah Yaya kabari inajin tsoro wlh nibantab'a yin wannan abunba dan ALLAH kark.... had'e bakinsu yyi gud'aya"yana zare vest d'in jikinsa...gaban baby na dukan Tara Tara tafara k'ok'arin k'watar kanta...amma gogan yyi nisa bayajin kira....✍️

b'angaren baby kuwa tana idar da girkin tayi wanka da sallar azahar"ta shirya cikin riga da siket"wanda iya gwiwa siket d'in yatsaya"rigar ta fito Mata da k'irar albarkatun kirjinta"tayi parking gashinta"bayan tayiwa jikinta wanka da turare"

B'angaren zuhura kuwa✍️
11/12/21, 8:22 AM - Ummi Tandama😇: Sun dade tare a falon bansan may suke zance a kai ba don ina daki tare dasu Aisha muna hira inda suke ban hiran abubuwan da hjy take yi masu na rashin dacewa gare su.
   Ban furta komai ba sai kara kwantar masu da hankali a kanta kamar yadda nima mamu take yawan yi min nasiha da kada na nuna abun yana damuna don dan ta bazai ji dadin hakan ba idan na nuna.
    Rukkaiya kan cewa take ita kan gaskiya ba zata iya shanye wani abu a ranta ba don da gangan ake maka cin fuska don kawai danta yana auren ka sai ka zama abin ki ga mutum ?
   Sai bayan la,asar yaya habbib ya bugo waya yana fadawa matar shi ga driver nan a waje yana jiran su don yaran sun fara damuwa lokacin.
   Sai da suka shirya sun dadauki abinda suke so a dakin nawa ban hanasu don sun nuna ra,ayi akan abina nasan Allah zai musaya min da wanda ya fishi idan na basu da zuciya daya.
    Muka fito zuwa dakin hjy don su sallameta a zaune take lokacin tana shan ferfesun kaysn cikin rago.
  Nafisa tana tsaye a kanta da ruwa rike a hannun ta tana bata jin sallaman mu shigowa yasa suka mayar da hankalin su gare mu.
   Mun mata yaya jiki yayinda take dan dago kai tans fadin ashe kuna gidan nan har yanzu eh yanzu zamu koma don yaran nacan su damu mu dawo.
    Ke nan ba za,a kawo su su ganin basu ko don kuna gudana da yayan ko hjy ta fada tana kai cibin da take shan ruwan miyan a bakin ta.
  Can muka fara zuwa gaida daddy shine suka lekawa mama don sun saba da ita yasa muka barsu can tare da ita don lokacin an bugo waya ana wanan fitinan da safe.
   Na fahinci may Rukkaiya take nufi yasa nayi saurin fadin hjy Nafisa ashe kin shigo ban sani ba ?
   Tace ummm ke dai fatima na shigo dazun bayan azahar da nake jin abinda wayan nan marasa mutuncin sukayi yau .
  Don ni ina kuryan Amma ina barci banji me ke faruwa ba sai dana tashi Amma ke labarta min hakan.
   Wallahi naso na shigo a lokacin da yau sun kara raina kansu wallahi don wana karon ba zan kyalesu ba Allah.
   Umm tashi mu tafi Rukkaiya tace tana hararan gefen da Nafisa din take .
  Jin hakan nace Nafisa ke nan wa yan nan ai sai irin su yan duniya don babu mutunci a tare dasu ko kadan.
   Na fahinci babu shiri a tsakanun su da samira do  haka nake kokarin a rabu lafiya a dakin don yanzu sam bansan me yasa bana son tashin hankali ba ni.
    Ganin sun fara fita daga dakin yasa nace barin dawo Nafisa in baki labari ta amsa da ai ina nan don kilama a gidan nan zan kwana yau.
    Nace kice dai yau mune da bakuwa a gidan nan ashe mara masu Aisha baya nayi ina fadin  sai yaushe kuma zan ganku ?
   Muna nan ai tunda an kawo mu jiya ne don haka sai kin gaji da ganin mu har bakin mota na raka su suka tafi ni kuma na koma ciki.
   Dakin nayi niyar shiga zancen dana sama sunayi ya hanani shiga dakin Nafisa ce ke fadin ai nasha fada maki wallahi fatima tafi su kirki dan dama Aisha don ita bata fitowa fili ta tsulawa mutum tsiya kai tsaye.
  Uhumm ni kaina canzawan yarinyar nan yana ban mamaki don da farko na dauka makircin sune take min don mijin nata ya gani.
   Amma yanzu na fara sarewa da al,amarinta don yadda akai wanan maganan sabuwa ta tona min asirin duk abinda mukayi tare da ita amma bata canza min fuska ba saima goyon bayana da tayi a gurin.
  Jin hakan yasa na fasa shiga dakin naja kafana nabar kofan dakin zuwa part din mu zuciyana cike da tunanen maganan nasu.
   Lalai mamu tayi gaskiya da take fadin duk abinda zataiwa hjy bazata taba son mu a ranta ba don kiyayyan mu ya riga da ya shiga ranta ko mai kankare shi sai Allah.
   Tunanen ba zai kare ba na tashi zuwa gun maria don ganin halin da suke ciki don yinin ranan na barta da yaran tana dawainiya dasu.
   Na sameta tana goye da babana da yai barci a bayanta sai sultana da take shiryawa don fauziya tana bayi tanawa yaran wanka lokacin saboda yamma dayayi.
    Har zuwa lokacin da yaya ya dawo gida baya cikin walwala tare dashi na fahunci hakan ne ta hanyan rashin cin abincin dare da baiyi ba sai ruwan shayin daya sha ya kwanta a lokacin.
    Ban matsa da tambayan shi haka nazo na kwanta na tashi don da safe ma muryan shine a inda yake karatun kur,ani ya tayar dani .
  Kallon shi nake da mamaki da bai tayar dani inyi sallah ba a ranan haka kuma banji lokacin da akai sallah asuba ba a ranan .
  Ki tashi kiyi sallah lokaci yana kurewa sanyin ruwan sama ya hanaki tashi kiyi sallah akan lokaci.
  Ban iya magana ba lokacin don raina da nake jin babu dadi saboda rasa sallah asuba danayi a ranan.
    Ban tsaya dakin ba fita nayi zuwa dakina na shiga bandaki kodana idar da sallah ban daga ba wurin ina zaune sai faman tunanen rayuwa nake a raina.
   Merry ce ta fado min a rai tausayin ta ya kamani don raba da da mahaifi sai Allah nasan kuma wucen merry ta bar queen a kasan nan bawai ta bar zancen bane.
    Saidai matakin da zata dauka ne ban san yadda zasu kaya karshe ita da mijin nata ba a karshe.
   Mikewa nayi don jin ruwa ya kara saukowa a lokacin hakan yasani fita daga dakin zuwa wurin maria don in duba halin da suke ciki.
    A daki na same su bata yarda tabar yaran sun fito koda falo ba don ruwan da ake shekawa a safiyan .
    Tana ganina ta fara gaidani kamar yadda nake kiranta da maman kid's haka na ambata mata ina zama nake tambayan ta ya suka kwana tace lafiya sai dai Abbanane jikin shi da dare ya danyi zafi kadan.
   Tabashi magani da safen nan taji zazzabin ya sauka nace a jikin shi nace ai da bakuyi mai wanka ba yanzu.
  Ina daki har ta shirya yaran suka fito cin abincin sai lokacin na tuna ban leka hjy ba ranan .
  Koda naje na same shi dakin suna hira nayi sallama na shigo Nafisa tana kwance saman dogon kujera har lokacin tana barci bata tashi ba.
    Mama Asabe ne ke fadi  ai nace hjy yau bamu ganki ba tun dazun  dan  murmushi na sake a fuskana ina fadin Abbana ne baida lafiya yau na dan tsaya dubashi.
   May ya samu yaron kuma ta sake tambayana, hjy kuma tana fadin waye ake kira haka cikin yaran ?
   Bani kadai ba har yaya saida ya dan kalli mahaifiyar tashi don jin tambayan da tayi a lokacin.
    Kafin wani cikin mu ya bada amsa sai mama Asabe tace karamin wanda ake goyo shine da wanan sunan.
    Ikon Allah sunan shi wa ke nan ake kiran sa da Abba shiru nayi ina mamakin ta a raine sai shine ya bata amsa da Abubakar yake ai.
   Sunan mahaifinta ke nan ka sakawa yaron ashe ai na fada maki tun lokacin da aka haifeshi Abubakar na saka mai.
   Amma ai baka fada min sunan mahaifinta bane ka saka mai ko zaiyi magana mama Asabe ta amsa da fadin ko ba sunan shi bane tunda haka sunan yake sai ta kirashi da Abbanta don sunan mahaifintane haka ?
   Umhumm kawai hjy tace tana kawar da kai gefe daya ganin haka yasa na mike don barin dakin sai mama Asabe ke fadin.
  Allah dai ya bashi lafiya da dukkan musulmi baki daya muka amsa da amin ina barin dakin.
    Wanka na shiga na fito na tsaya shiryawa hakan ne ya dan daukeni lokaci har yakai ga shigowa dakin ban gama shiriba.
    Fuskana babu walwala ina mai jin zafin maganan hjy datayi kan yarona ko adduan samun lafiya bai samu ba a gurin ta.
    Yana shigowa ya kalleni ganin yanayi na yake fadin ni zan fita akwai inda zamu tafi da Nasir yau.
   Kayi break ne na tambaya daga inda nake zaune a gaban mirrow na yace yanzu dai zan fita nayi ina son zanje gida yau in gaida kawu na fada.
    Ki bari sai idan na samu lokaci mu shiga tare don ina son zuwa mu zauna dashi nima amma kasan mamu bataji dadi ba tun shekaran jiya ban lekasu ba .
    Kina magana kamar wace ke cikin bacin rai mana ta fada a daidai lokacin daya kai kofan dakin .
   A kan me raina zai baci  zancen mama kikewa wanab daure fuskan kaima a kanshi zaka hanani zuwa gida na tambaya.
   Kafin ya bada amsa nace akan me zan daure fuska tunda bani tayiwa maganan haka ba kuma ai da zaka saka sunan bakai shawara dani ba da sai ince a saka na kawuna.
   To ni na saka na surikina amma ai ba aso ka saka din ba ya dan kalleni ya girgiza kai yana fadin Allah dai ya raya min yarana yasaka masu albarka.
   Ya juya ya fita bin bayan shi nayi da kallo tare da jin wani takaici a raina naso yau in je gida in duba su mu dan kebe da mamu kan abubuwan da suka faru.
    Ban fito ba don na karbi dana muka kwance tare a dakin haka yasa ban fito ba sai wajajen sha biyun rana ko shi sultana ce ta shigo da kuka dakin na falka.
 Muka fito tare zuwa falon lokacin ne maria ke fada min kamar taga merry ta shigo gidan nan don taga wasu mata sun fito part din ta dazun.
   A raina nace nasan za a rina shigowan Atika ya dauke min hankali ban kara bi ta kan zancen merry din ba koda gaske ne ita din ce.
  Don Atika dana gani saboda koda mukazo kasan tun lokacin mun samu tana bauchi sunyi bukin kanen mijin ta su biyu a can.
   Mun jima da Atika a dakin muna hira kafin mu fito zuwa cin abinci a falo sai lokacin na tabbatar da zuwan merry din da muke kwankwanton zuwan ta garin.
    Da fara, a a fuskana na yi mata sannu da zuwa ta amsa min da kyat ganin haka yasa na share ta don ni ba lokacin ta nake dashi ba yanzu.
  Ban kuma koma dakin su hjy ba don saboda cin fuskan da tayi min saboda kawai sunan mahaifina dataji yaron dashi.
   Bayan mun gama cin abinci na kai Atika dakin don ta gaida ita da jiki suna zaune sunyi shiru a dakin da gani kowan su yayi zurfi a tunanen wani zance ne muka shigo dakin.
    Sai mama Asabe ke fadin kai bukar ya jikin naje dubashi akace kuna barci tare dashi a dakin.
   Na dan juya ina kallon ta tare da fadin yaji sauki ai mama nagode komay hjy ta gani can tace an fada ma maza na rakin ciwo ne halan mai rakin ciwo.
   Dan murmushi nayi kawai ina fadin wanan kawatace tun na yarinta yanzu suna garin nan ne da mijin ta zaune.
    Ohh itama yar bauchin kuce ashe Nafisa da tunda muka shigo sai a lokacin tayi magana take fadan haka.
   Na dan juya inda take ina fadin kin tashi ke nan don iyakata da ita dan guntun murmushi kawai tunda na fahinci tsanata a garesu har zuciyan su yake.
   Ashe merry ta dawo ne Nafisa ta fada  yanzu na tashi na ganta nima na bata amsa a takaice muka daga muka bar dakin.
    Karfe biyar bayan Atika ta tafi ne sai gashi ya dawo wanan karon su ukunsu suka shigo gidan inda suka yada zango a dakin da mahaifiyar su take.
    Sun dan jima a dakin shi ya fara fitowa ya same ni a daki tare da fauziya muna ba Abba magani don jikin shi da ya kara zafi lokacin..
   Har yanzu jikin nasa da zafi ke nan ya fada lokacin daya shigo yana karasowa inda muke .
  Na dan dago kai ina fadin yanzu ya kara zafi kuma shine nake bashi magani .
  Dukawa yayi ya karbi yaron a hannu na yana fadin bari ayi sallah mu kaishi asibiti a dubashi bandai yi magana ba har ya gama da yaron zai fita nake fadin ashe merry tana tafe ne ?
   Merry ya juyo da mamaki yana tambaya fuskan shi dauke da mamakin jin haka.
   Tazo ne ya tambaya yana kallon fuskana da mamaki sai kuma ya juya yana fadin humm ya fita.
  Nikan sai ban san dalilin jin zuwanta bai kwantar min da hankalina ba don haka nake faman kafa kafa da yarana a gidan na saka ido ga duk wani motsi nasu.
   Zuwan ta da kamar sati biyu a gidan kowa na zaman sa ba ruwan wani a tsakanin mu don baka sanin ma tana gidan sai idan ta fito za a ganta.
   Gashi ta rame ta kara duhu sosai da gani bata cikin yanayin dadin rai yanzu.
   Kai ranan dai yawan mafalkin da nakeyi marasa dadi yasa na yanke hukunci fadawa mamu cikin daren nake waya da mamu ina fada mata nifa hankalina bai kwanta da zuwan merry din nan ba, .
   Don haka kawai nake jin faduwan gaba tunda na ganta garin nan ga yawan mafarki da nakeyi mara dadi.
   Mamu ta tambayeni da ita kike yawan mafalkin kodawa nace hankalina daine bai kwanta ba mamu.
   Nasan halin bakaken fatan America wurin daukan famsa sai nake ganin kamar shi tazo yi a kan yarana da sauri mamu tace
  Don  me zaki fadi haka sayadi ina ruwanki da maganan su da zata dauki fansa gun yaran ki kuma.
   Mamu nidai hankalina bai kwanta ba da wanan matar tunda har naji gabana yana faduwa haka tunzun ta gaskiya a cikin daren nan zan kawo su yanzu daya shiga sallah.
  Ki bari zanzo da kaina tare da Nura mu dauko su a daidai lokacin ta yadda ba wanda zai gane hakan zan ma kawun ki bayanin komai yanzu.
 Inji mai zai fada kada in kwaso yaran bada sanin shi ba.
    Haka kuwa ya faru don ba wanda yasan fitan su gidan har da maria suka fice gidan koda ya dawo yana tambaya cewa nayi kawu ya aiko aka kwashe su dazun bayan fitan shi.
    Don me aka dauke su da yamman nan nace ban sani ba ya dai aiko yana son yaran su zo mai  ne kawai yace.
  To amma kuma idan daddy zai kwashi yaran zai kira ko ya fada amma haka ka, , ,  sai kuma yai shiru ya juya ya fice daga dakin.
   Nikan ganin merry tana gari yasa nake rufe kofana da wuri na shige abina daki in kwanta.
     Da kyat na samu Abbana yayi barci ranan sai fitina yake min bayan na samu yayi barci, bayan dan fitinan daya taba shiga barcin can cikin barcina nake jin hayani ana dukan kofana da karfi kafin in bude idona da kyau ina saurare kofan aka kara duka da karfi sosai na mike zaune ina kokatin kunna autan dakin don inga haske.

saiyaje gidan yaga baby da ameer"kuma baya zuwa hannu biyu"abban baby kuwa yafad'awa ummi" ranar da baby tayi 40 zata koma kosun huta da zaryar da dr hakeem ke musu...
